Izinju. Ngeli xesha kwakuxa lijikayo ilanga lemini yehlobo,

ukundisa khona ngokwawo.

IMVUSELELO NOMQUKUMBELO.
Kuthe emveni koku zakha zema iintlanganiso, kwahamba
iimvuselelo ezinkulu zeLizwi. Ibe ngumjikelo omnye ukususela
baye beguquka abantu, bezixela izono zabo ; yaye impi yasema-
ngomnye umhla kwiinvuselelo enkulu eyayingomhla weNkosi,
izixela ukuba ingumbulali wabantu, bonke ubom bayo,—ixele
nokuba yayikho mini kwadutyulwa ezinye izihange ezibini
ngumLungu ababezama ukumbada kuyo le ntsungusi yase-
Mgudu. Inqike indoda leyo nezinye izehlo eyayikade ibaphaka-
ukusinda yona. Iwuchukushe kakuhle umhla ababeza kubhada
undwendwe ngohambelo lwalo lokuqala, basuke bonelwa liLawo,
iyandiqonda kakuhle; namhlanje iyabulahla ubume bayo
noko sezihambile iintsuku zayo.
Kwesi sithuba iindwendwe zifumene kuyimfanelo ukuba
mazijike zisinge emakhaya azo. Kodwa kube mhlophe ukuba
benzini le iqaliweyo apha, ngohlobo lokuba akungabisalunga
ukuyishiya. Bathe ke ngoko ukugoduka kwabo bona yasel* iba
kukuya kulungisa impahla yabo, nokumisa izinto zasemakhaya
ngendlela, baze bajike beze kuba ngabantu basemaRanugeni.
Ibe ngemvumelwano entle le ndawo ; sathi nathi noko silusizi

ndisi, kanti hayi kukuphaphamela izinto eziluncedo lwesizwe
kwabemi balo ; kukuzincama kwabantu balo njengoko isibhalo
umphefumlo wakhe wowulahla ; kodwa othe wawulahla umphe-
Iindaba eziza zivela kuloo macala ngoku zifika futhi, zave
zimnandi, kwaye kubonakala ukuba inkqubela-phambili iqinile,
•mfundo inzulu, injalonje iphakamile. Woqonda umfundi xa
ndithe ziphaya ngoku iincutshe ezivela kwelamajapani, kwelama-
naseAbhisiniya, nezeli lizwe kakade. Iinqanawa zorwebo seziha-
mba kula akufuphi amazibuko. Kuthiwa indlala kwelo yalityalwa
ukuba iynto ebikhe ibekho. Kuthiwa abantu bayaphulana
ubutyebi obulapho abunakuxelwa, ade athi lawa madoda abetha
kwalila ibhungane ukuyiyila kwawo le misebenzi ebuphantsini,
kumacandelo ayo amabini, kuba seyineminyaka emithathu
iphakathi kobuzwe.

benzi. Iphepha lendaba alibanga linye ngoku, aye lawa aphambili

ndiso, neenkuthazo zawo azenza ebuzweni.
Ibe sisiseko esihle kunene othe wasekeka phezu kwaso lo mzi,
kwanelizwe elo liphela. Bakhuthala abemi balo, bawaphaphame-
nobulumko, nempilo entle yabantu, neyempahla ehambayo;
babuthanda ubuzwe babo, nentetho vabo, nenkonzo yeLizwi.
Ngaphaya kwako konke oku, bazuze neyona nto idla ngoku-
nqabela nezona zizwe zinamandla,—*  Umanyano nemvisiswano.’

11. Izohlwayo.—EMbusweni wesiXhosa wemini zamzuzu,
kweli lizwe litsha mncinane umsebenzi wayo ; kuba inikelwe
kwinto esezigcinwe Komkhulu ngokuncameka.
1. Intombi ephambe abafazi yabonwa seyonakele, kakade
mayitshatiswe naye loo mfana ; kodwa phambi kokutshatiswa
intombi le iya kuya kwisohlwayo ekuthiwa, —‘ Kukunqazelwa,
nomfana lowo uya kusiwa kwakuso. Esi sohlwayo sinje :—
Kukho amadoda amakhulu, abuso bugwanyalala, akumashumi
isithuba esithile, eyijongile, ethe cwaka.
Umfana lo naye kwelinye igumbi ejongwe ziintokazi ezinkulu
2. Umfazi obanjwe nomnye umfo enendod’ akhe, uya ku-
nani. Ikrexe elo lihlawuliswe. Kuze kwenziwe isidlo kwindawo
Umntu wokuqala oya kutya kweso sityo atye yedwa ejongiwe
ngabo bantu yoba nguye loo mfazi.
3. Indoda ebulele enye indoda kumka-yo ; nendoda ebulele
umntu emfumene eyantaza ngokwenyangaza emzini wayo
mana ukuya kuzibonakalalisa Komkhulu ngazo zonke iinyanga
4. Umfana othe enomka-khe wonakalisa intombi, eyiphinda
nokuba uyayiqala loo ntlondi, uya kufuduswa kunye nosapho
zaKomkhulu, nokuba ngamahlathi, ide iminyaka ibe sixhenxe,
ubomi bakhe bonke enomvuzo olungileyo ngokomsebenzi wakhe.
5. Isela maliyihlawule ngokuphindiweyo loo nto liyibileyo,
kunye neendleko zokusetyenzwa kwetyala lalo. Ukuba liphindile
maliyihlawuje ngokuphindwe kane. Ngokwesithathu malifudu-
kugcina amadama ezintabeni, livuzwre kakuhle ; kodwa isiqinga-
ukwakha izindlu zetolongo.
6. Umntu ogebenge omnye ngenzondo nenqala, makagwe-
makafuduke kunye nentsapho yakhe aye kuba ngumqali wama-
bakuzo.
7. Indoda enentsimi engange akile enye, kulindeleke ukuba
ukufikelela enanini uRulumente makayithathe ngokwakhe loo
ntsimi okomnyaka, ayinike isahlulo indoda leyo, ayibuyisele
kunye nentsimi yayo, ayilungiselele. Ukuba iphindile yenza
ubufede, mayilinywe loo ntsimi kwanguRulumente ngesahlulo,
8. Utywala abunamthetho ; buselwa ngumntu ngokuzitha-
ndela, busenziwa emzini wakhe lusapho lwakhe ; kodwa angaze
ukuthengisa imithombo. ObesiLungu utywala abuvunyelwa
ukungena kuyo le mida ; kanti noko uyazenzela elowo ngediliya
9. Umntu ofunyenwe elele enxilile makathwalelwe emzini
wamageza kunye kade elilo naye. Ayigqibe iveki ephethwe ngo-
kwegeza, enxibe ngokufanayo nawo. Ephindile anikwe inyanga.
10. Izingcolo ezinjengombulo, fudula iyinto echaseke kunene
kukutshatiswa umtshato ongangcwaliswanga ngumfundisi, be-
mkiswe ke baye kuba ngabasebenzi kwiindawo ezikude, apho
kuqingqwa imithi, kuhluzwe namayeza, boba lapho iminyaka
(onentsapho) esimba amatye okwakha imizi yaKomkhulu,
iiOfisi neetolongo.
Mayanele le ibhaliwevo okwakaloku ; imininzi yona imimiselo
nemithetho yokuhlala njengesizwe.

kha basebenza ngayo bamdlula. Wayephantsi kwemeko ebuhlu-
ngu yokungaphili phantse mihla le. Wayebhaqisa ukuba aphile
qete. Amandla omzimba aya esetha kodwa wazingisa ngokukha-
mfundisi waseDyoki kwakufuthi eshumayela nakwiirhamente
zabaMhlophe. Enyanisweni abamelwane bakhe xa aphakathi
kwabo babelibala nokuba umnyama. Babemamkela njengoko
wayenjalo engqondweni nasentliziyweni nasemphefumlweni,
indoda yenene, indoda yamadoda.

kwabantu bakowabo.

UKUVELA NOKUTSHONA.
Ngomhla we-15 kwinyanga yoKwindla, nyakana ngemofu
(15th March 1855,) kwakungathi kuncinane xa kuthiwa, uLena,
umkaCholwephi, ongumolokazana kaBokwe, into yasema-
Bambeni, kwaKrila, uzele umntwana oyinkwenkwe, kuloo ntla-
njana ingena eTyhume, eDikeni, kuthiwa ukubizwa kwayo ngu-
Ntselamanzi. Lo mntwana uyinkwenkwe ke nguye lo ikho ngaye
kwesizwe nesizukulwana sakhe, njengoko la machaphazana
Zithi izithethi zintathu iintsuku zokukhungwa komntu (1)
kwakhe ukuba ngumntu oyinkazana,) (3) lusuku lokubhubha
kwakhe. Zintathu njalo ezo ntsuku, kuthi ngamaxesha afana nala
nwe zezo ntsuku zontathu ekwilizwe elinye. Lo mfo ke sibalisa
ngaye, uzifumene zweni linye zozithathu ezo zikhungo; kuba esi
Ntselamanzi, ngomhla wama-22 kwinyanga yoMqungu ngo-
mnyaka we-1922. (22nd February 1922), emzini wakhe.
Umntu wasemzini owayekho kwinkonzo yomn cwabo, obenga-
mazi umfundisi lo, wayeya kude abuze ukuba lo mntu ube yintoni
na le nto inkonzo yomngcwabo wakhe inje ? Ubuya kuthi waku-
buza ukuba uthetha ukuba njani na, asuke afumane akhohlwe
kukuyenza inkcazelo yento engaphakathi kuye,—kanti umanga-
Kuthe ngosuku lokutshona komphakathi lo, into leyo eyenzeka
zalala izigidimi zeenyawo, neencwadi, neengcingo, zajuba kuma-
zwe akude nakufuphi. Zithe iimpendulo zakwanjalo ukukhawule-
za ukufika, kangangokuba ngosuku olulandela olo, zibe iincwadi
neengcingo sezizizithungu, zize kuxela umothuko nokukhuza
usapho ; baye abantu abaze kwenza imbeko yabo yokugqibela
kumfi, sebewasazela.

qhula amxela ubunto bakhe emisebenzini, nemibutho abeba pha-
kathi kwayo, zawutsho ezo nto wambejembeje ngokungakumbi.

no ezingemilibo yokuzalwa; kodwa abavangeli uMateyu no-

kuphela ndawo ukulandwa kwemilibo yokuzalwa, ngakumbi
Njengoko sesitshilo, uMfundisi ujohn Knox Bokwe uzalwa

kaBokwe ophakathi. Unina womfundisi lo ngumNtakwenda-

kwaseDikeni. Emva koQono kuzelwe ujacob Cholwephi; kuze
uzele uCandlish inkulu yakhe, aze uCandlish azale uMelville
nabaninawa bakhe neentombi ezithile. UMelville (Honono)
uzele uGladstone nabanye.

kukuze seyinikwa igama lokuba ngu“ Cholwephi. ”

kaHintsa, ngeempembelelo zikaRev. Dr. John Philip, umfundisi

njalo,—wabuya ke ngoko uMaqoma waya kwakwelo wayegqo-

kuDaliwe (Cathcart).
Kuthe ukuxola kwemfazwe kaHintsa iRhuluneli yathumela
indodana efanelekileyo ukuba ize kumisa ikampi eDikeni, iphathe
ulawulo kweso sithuba sokulungiswa kwezinto. Igama laloo
ndawo kwathiwa ngesiLungu yiBlock Drift, nantso phantsi
kwehospitile yaseLovedale. Igama lendodana leyo kvvakuthiwa
nguCaptain Charles Lennox Stretch; emaXhoseni waziwa
ngegama lokuba ngu“ Xolilizwe
besusa umfundi wabo okholekileyo, onguFiti uyise kaTshuka
ukuba aye kubacelela inxowa kuMaqoma, uthe yena waya ngaku-
Noyi (Balfour) uyise kaMakhaphela, umphakathi wakwaMaqoma,
nobelikholwa likaNtsikana. Uthe uMaqoma akuva ukuba
mpumalanga koGaga, wabathumela kuTyhali, esithi lowo mhlaba
Makucace kumfundi ukuba uTyhali lo noMaqoma ngoonyana
bakaNgqika, ukumkani weli lizwe, koko bobabini abangabo
mkani esikhundleni sikayise, eseXesi, esemncinane ; aba ingama-

nesithethi. UTyhali wayelixhiba, eyindoda ezolileyo, enohloni,
UTyhali nguyise kaNgonyama (Oba) noFeni (Dos) imiNgca-
kwabo lezizwe ezimhlophe, ngakumbi amaNgesi; kuba athi
uNgqika, aba bantu ngabakokwabo, watsho wabakhuphela le
ndlu yakuloTyhali, wathi ngamabandla akoNibe,—uNonibe ke
ngunina kaTyhali. Waqokela umfo kaMlawu uNgqika, wabasikela
umhlabakazi omkhulu eNgqakayi (Fort Wiltshire), wathi maba-
ke nantso kwaseDikeni, phakathi kwalo neNgqushwa.
Ngelifutshane abefundisi baye bawufumana umhlaba kuTyhali,
nakuba imida ingandulanga yenziwe ; imbali ithi umhla woku-
miswa kwemida, nokubhalwa kwezigqibo waba wodwa,—yaba
ngumhla ongemncinane lowo,—bekho nabefundisi eDikeni,
wehla noMaqoma weza eDikeni, noTyhali ekhona ; ikomkhulu
“Siyawunikela lo mhlaba ukuba kufundiswe kuwo oonyana bethu
ube lilifa kubo, kude kube sisizukulwana sabo,—siyazi ukuba
uXolilizwe uya kuyenza loo nto.” Ingcwaba likaXolilizwe
(Capt. C. L. Stretch) likumhlaba wakwaSomaseti.

UKUZALWA NOKUKHULA.
Uthe lo mzi, mtsha wamaMbalu namajingqi, nemiNgcanga-
zesikolo, waluncedo kwinto yonke engumsebenzi owenziwa
ngabefundisi. Kwathi kusekwakheni izindlu kwabe kuseku-
nqwelo, kwabe kusekutyaleni iintango zamakhala nezekwepila.
Inxenye yabo ithande ukuya kuma kwaNtselamanzi, isithuba
ndisi lo, ephakathi kwamakowabo, nooyisekazi, ooTshuka no-
Dungela,—kuba uyise uBokwe akahlalanga kanjalo, ugaleleke
noMnyaluza ngemfazwe yamaLinde, (1818) ekwakuvakala
ukuba aseGqili. Ujube kwelo umfo kaNgxogu, wahlala iminya-
ka, wade wabhubhela kwelo zwe.
Njengoko sesitshilo kwisahluko sokuqala uzalelwe apho ke
umfundisi lo, kwaNtselamanzi, ngomhla we-15 kwinyanga
igama elikhulu lomfundisi owayeyinkokeli yebandla eSkotilani,
—ujohn Knox ; waba ke ngoko waziwa kowabo ngelokuba ngu-
Ngaloo mihla bekungekho mfuneko yokuba athi umntwana
ndo ; ngokunjalo ke uNokisi lo ude waminyaka isibhozo nesi-
mjelweni eLovedale; kusakuthiwa kuseStation School, kuba
bhela eMgqakhwebe, phantsi kwaMahlathi (Pirie), engumkho-
keli weBandla.
Ngomnyaka we-1853 kuqalile ukuba kumiswe iOfisi eDikeni,
bathi ubudolophana obo banikwa igama lokuba yiAlice, sabe
isithili esi kusithiwa sesaseVictoria East. Igama lokuba athi
ko nokuba zininzi izinto ezibe zithethwa ngalo elo dike, lisoyikeka
kuba oko laliphakathi kwamatyholo nokunqaba, laye liphezu
Ithe yakuqalwa iOfisi, iRhuluneli yacela umfundisi kaMaqoma
ukuba abe yimantyi kweso sithili, yaye imcisha ngenxa yokuba
nethuba phakathi kwabantu abamnyama,—igama lomfundisi
Uthe kwa-oko wacela ujacob Bokwe ukuba abe likhumsha lakhe
ngamaSkotshi kuloo tyalike yawo yayiqala ukwakhiwa eAlice,
ukuba ngumhlabeli kuyo ngemihla yenkonzo, kuba ngezo mini
iihadi zazingekabi ngaka.
Ithe kanti iyafunda inkwenkwana uNokisi esikolweni, njengo-
kuba imana ukuya ibuya eLovedale. Ibisakuthi ngamaxa
wambi ithunywathunywe zintwanantwana ngabefundisi aba
baphakathi komzi apha, yathi ngoko yanokuwazi umzi lo, yano-
kuqabuka nokuphaphama ; akwaze kubekho mfundisi unasikrokro
kuthiwa yayizuziswa ixabiso lehafugolweni (2/6) ngenyanga.
Ngelo xesha ubuhlanti beenkomo zabefundisi, babukule ndawo
inendlukazi yokufundela, ekwayityalike yomzi (New Building).
ukuze ade ange ungunyana nje kuye ? Ukuwuphendula kwakhe
ukuba kukho umfundisi omtsha oza kufika eLovedale, noko loo
nto ayibanga sengqondweni kuyaphi kubo. Kude kwathi
ngamhla uthile ekungeneni komnyaka we-1867 ; emva kwemvu-
beziintwana zontathu, inguye, inguBryce Balfour, noDaniel
kwamanzi kwaGaga, aye ayede atyhobozela nakwesi sitalato
nohadi walufunda khona.
IMFUNDO NOKUZAKHA.
Imfundo alifumaneki ixabiso layo, ide ibe ifike kumntu obe-
nengqondo yakhe yemvela ; ixabiseka kakhulu kanjalo imfundo
ingcwalisekile, yaqaqamba, xa ithe yafika kumntu onothando
ukuba asiyiyo yakhe, yeyesizwe sakhe,—into yakhe yena luzuko
ngayo, yayithabatha ngaloo ndlela kanye imfundo yayo, ayabi yi-
“ ibhongo.” Xa umntu kuthiwa unezi zinto zombini esiXhoseni
usukuba enganconywa; phofu “ igugu ” kukubuka loo nto
intsha uthe wanayo, ne“ bhongo ” kukucinga ngento, okanye
ngumnqweno wokunqwenela into ongayifikeleliyo, osaya kubuye
uyifikelele mhlawumbi ekuhambeni kwexesha, ngokuyenzela
Ngako oko, siyabona ngoku, ngale mihla, ukuba ezi zinto zo­
mbini “ igugu ” ne“ bhongo ” zizinto ezilunge kanye kumntu
ukuze isebenze, kuhle kanjalo ukuba umntu ofundileyo abe
namabhongo ngemfundo yakhe, acinge izinto aya kuzenzela
isizwe ngayo, wothi ke mhlawumbi abethe nganeno kwebhongo
nyana bakhe besebancinane, ukholisa ukuphumelela, ngaphezu
kwendoda engabanga namagugu namabhongo ngabayo oonyana.
Umfundisi lo sibhala ngaye kule ncwadana siyakholwa ukuba
ubenazo ezi ndawo zonke sizikhankanyileyo, okanye imfundo
njengoko iintlobo ngcentlobo zababhaleli zithe zabonakalisa
kuxa ithe yafika kumntu ozithobileyo, athi oko kuzithoba kwakhe
igxumekeke ke yona ithi ngxi. Ikratshi yinto ecekiseka kakhulu,
naphi na naphi na; kodwa likratshi into egcina umntu ukuba
izixela ubuni obubo. Likho eli lokuzidla ngegama likayihlo,
ungathandi ukuba ligqubeke eluthulini, eli ebesakuthi umfo
uve kukhuzwa kusithiwa : “ Yint’ enjen’ ikratshi! ”
ngoku imfundo yakhe, uya kuzama ngokwakhe ukuzakha ngayo.
Iya kumchitha imphalaze ukuba uyekele kuyo, ide iphelele eku-
beni imenzakalise. Ihashe eli linento engummangaliso,—nje-
ngokuba ligcinwe ngomkhala nje ngumkhweli walo, lithi ukuba
ngendlela egodukayo,—hayi liya kuphambuka ; liphambuke liye
ngamandla etyholweni, nokuba kuseliweni, nokuba kusezindo-
ngeni, nokuba kuphi na apho kuya kuba yingozi yalo kunye
nalowo uhleli phezu kwalo. Loo nto ke yenziwa nalihashe
njwe ngomkhala, ethi kwakuyekelwa kuyo kungabikho kwakheka.
Umkhala ke ekuyalezwa wona futhi-futhi ngamadoda ayalayo,
ngumkhala oliLizwi. Kuthiwa iLizwi likaThixo lithambi-
ukuzithoba lowo lifike kuye, ukuze linyuke ke Iona, kanti ngoku-
nyuka kwalo kukunyuka kwakhe naye. Athi ngokuthamba
kokuzithoba lifumane ke ingcambu lime ngxi lingawiswa ziza-
qhwithi nemisinga ; kanti ke nguye lowo umiyo ungawiyo.
Kukho enye into eyalezwayo nguMpostile uPawulos; ndine-
ntloni ukuthi siyikhangela kancinane thina bantu beli xesha loo
nto, asiyithatheli ngqalelo; kuthe ke ngenxa yoko yasidla bukhwa-
basa, yasinqunqa yasigqiba. Loo nto ke ndithetha ubungca-
ke le ncwadana, ndinemihlali ukuthi le ndoda sibhala ngayo ibiyi-
ngcathu ngezinto zonke. ILizwi ilamkele isengumntwana,
kukuthi ngegama elinye, indoda le yafunda yafunda, yanqaba,
izinqabisa ngokwayo,—yathi ngoko kunqaba yazuza ukwakheka,
ngenxa yeLizwi.
Umfundi uya kuqonda ukuba ngomnyaka we-1869, xa kanye
umfundisi lo ayinkwenkwana eminyaka ishumi nye linesine (14)
ubudala, kweso sithuba kwakungekabikho nto iyitempile kweli
kubekho iBandla labaZili Benene kwelasemaXhoseni, anganeli
kwenjenjalo, koko ade abambe iindawo eziphambili zobuZili
Benene, ade afa afileyo engazange abe nokukrokreleka. URev.

Kuthe ngoNovember (inyanga yeNkanga) ngomnyaka we-1897,

kwaphendulwana, kwayalezwana, kwabuliswana. Umhla we-
zo ntetho yayingowe-13 kwinyanga yoMnga (13th Dec. 1897).

ngomnyaka we-1909, wazigibisela wonke uMr. Bokwe kuso;

Bokwe lo ukhulele phakathi kwamagqala akowabo abembalisela
kunene ngoNtsikana lo. Uthe ke yena kwakwiimini zamzuzu

ngoma zakhe. Uthe ke namhla akuwubona umzi wakowabo
uwasabela amalinga akhe, wakunye nawo naye.

Krestu. Athe uMr. Bokwe wenziwa umbhali ojikelezayo.

kwelakwaBhaca, kumaXesibe, maMpondweni, nakumaMpondo-
mise, ziye ngomndilili omkhulu, kwiintlanganiso eziseKoloni,

njana, zibe zikho nezilungiselelwe abantu bonke.

netheko ekukulo, nahlalayo ezingqondweni zabantu abatsha.

ibe yimigudu ukunqandwa kwezinqandelwa ekhaya.

ukuba ngenye imini ndiya kuhlanganisehva kwabakowethu.

balive igama eli, baze banqwenele ukuva ukuba ngumntu
umsebenzi wakhe ? Evelaphi na ? Waza wemka esishiva
nantoni na sona isizukulvvana nesizalwane sakhe, kwa-
kunye nabantu bakowabo, njalo-njalo. Ngayo ke le
migcana ingeyakoni, ndihlangabeza loo minqweno.

ngokungazilungiseleli ngokumalunga nenkqubela-phambili
nentlalo entle, namafa, njalo-njalo ; ndithi:—“ Bantwana
bam ! Intlalo yethu yayingeyiyo yobomi, yaye iyeyokufa;
iyingxovu emnyama, engenamva namphambili ! Nokuba
uThixo uSomandla makabulelwe.”
nala mabona-ndenzile obu bomi, abe aya kuba sisilumkiso,
nemfundiso, nesiyalekiso kuni.

UKumiwa koMhlaba.
Kukho imbuzwano enkulu ngale mihla yethu, malunga

wavo ukuba iza kwenza ntoni na.

kuba sisixakaniso na. Ukuba sikho, wobona enye indawo;
kuthi ukuba asikho agxumeke.

KWACENTANE.
Ngomnyaka we-1885, okuthiwa ngesiXhosa ngumnyaka
“ weNdlala yoMphunzisa,” okanye ngumnyaka wesi-
“Twayi,” phakathi kuwo loo mnyaka, ufudukile ubawo
uNzanzana, kwaNgede, kwaCentane, phesheya kweNciba,
kwelakwaGcaleka. UBawo esenjenjeya nje, ushiya uyise
uKrune apha eKrwakrwa, eTyhume ; koko ucinge Iowa
uyise wayemkhokelela eseyinkwenkwre.
Ngelo xesha mna ndiminyaka isithoba, ndiphethe oweshu-
mi ndikho. Be kusithi oko, xa kuthethwa ngelizwe lakwa-
Gcaleka, uqonde ukuba umntu akathethi ngalizwe ; ade
zisekho khona.
Nam bendisakuqhulwa futhi ngabantu ; bebona uko­
nwaba kwam njengomntu oza kuya kwizwe elinjalo. Be-
ndisakubuzwa ukuba iziqhamo ndizithanda kangakanje,
iilamuni, amafiya, iipesika neekwepila ndiya kuzifumana-
ukuba akafuduki nathi yena; woza abuye alandcle.
Kuhambe mna ke nobavvo neentwazana zakowethu ezimbi-
ni ; kuxa ziphethe ishumi elinesithandathu enkulu kuzo,
Nzanzana, osisibonda saloo ngingqi. Saye sithwelwe
iindawo kwangaphaa kulaa mazwe aseKoloni.”
Ndiyanakana ukuba nathi safika ilizwe lilibi jikelele ;

ngaphambili, nangani wayengumfo osisithuli ; kuba lici-
ngwe ithuba elide. Liphuma nje, liphuma selojiwe
Makwanele xa ndithi, yawudlavula umzi loo ndlala yaloo
mnyaka—indlala eyaziwa ngegama lokuba yindlala yo-
Mphunzisa. Umphunzisa ke ngumthi abada abantu bemba
wona bawutya ; kuba phaya ezingcanjini zawo kukho isi-
gaqa esingangethang,a, ekube kusetyenzwa sona sityiwe,
noko bekungekho bumnandi banto.
Kwavakala izinto ngezinto ezimbi, zokubiwa kweempa-
kwezisele zokutya ; nokuqhekezwa kweevenkile. Mandi-
kwafika indyebokazi engenganganto.
Kwakuso eso sithuba saloo ndlala yoMphunzisa,
iinkomo nazo zingenelwe yinto engaziwayo, ekuthiwa
ukubizwa kwayo, sisitwayi. Ibisakusuka inkomo ibhitye
ibe lugogo, ube umzimba lo ukhuthuke wathi bo. Loo
nto ke izele ngamakhalane nemikhaza, neendlanga.
Iindlebe ezi ziyopha, zithi xhi ; bude ngamaxa wambi
ubuhlanti obu bube nevumba xa iinkomo zikhoyo. Kwa-
ibisuka itsho ngomchitho obomvu, nomzimba lo wayo
uzale ligazi. Zafa kakhulu iinkomo seso sifo.
Zithe ezi zifo zawaxaka kakhulu amaNgqika, kuba aye-
nezi zifo zempahla, zatsho kwaba ngakumbi. Laye ilizwe
qwane novivingane ekutyeni, ezo nto zingaqhelisi.

kudlala, phofu nako kukwaneenduma ezophayo, namanxe-
ba, nemivumbo, neengongoma.
Kwiimfeketho zala makhwenkwe aMaqaba ke, ezinje
ngokugibisela inkuku yomntu ; nokudwaba intsimi yomntu
nawo ngoku, ngaphezu kokundihlonela. Akathanda
ukuba imihlambi yethu idibane ; nam ndakholwa, kuba
bendithanda ukutyisa emadlelweni anohlaza, mna ukuze
ndive ubumnandi xa ubaw* omkhulu athi, “ Uyindoda
wena ! ” atsho ngokubona ukuhlutha kwezakowethu, ibe
ilambile eminye imihlambi.
Kuthe ngenxa yokuthenjwa kwam ngubaw’ omkhulu
ngasempahlen’ apha, nokubuza kum yonke into esisonaka-
ndarola amahlahla, ndabiya, endibone ngathi iyafa,
ndiyiseze. Ezi zinto sendizenza ngegunya lokuba ndiza
kubuzwa ukuba, “ Ubuthembiselana nabani na ngale nto?”

mbazana, lokuba mandingathunywa zinto-yinto zase-
ndlwini, hleze ndiphulukane nempahla. Loo nto ke
ndayithabathel’ apha, ndimalele umfazi endithuma, ekha-

iintwanazana zakokwethu ezimbini ? Hee, ubawo waya
kuba seRini yena kwanini. Senditshilo kambe ukuthi,
inkonzo yaseRabe ; waphathelwa phezulu ke ngoko ! Azi
umfo ufundile ? Azi umfo uyasithetha isiLawu ? Waba

Iintwanazana zakokwethu zikho apha kunye nam. Azi-

Umthetho wamaXhosa ubonakalisa ubulungisa nokulungela ngendlela ekhululekileyo. Iinkosi zakwaXhosa zazilawula ngobulungisa ngaphandle kokubulala abantu.
UMqhayi wabonakalisa ubuchule bakhe ekubhaleni ngemithetho yamaXhosa. Le ncwadi yaba ngumzekelo wendlela yokulawula ethi ibe ngowabantu bonke.
Abafundi bayithanda le ncwadi ngoba yayibaxhomekele kwindlela yabo yokulawula. Ityala lamawele liyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu.
Indlela yokulawula kwamaXhosa yayixhomekeke kubulungisa nokulungela. Umthetho wayo wayebopha ubulungisa obungaphandle kwabamhlophe.
UMqhayi wathi xa ebhala le ncwadi wafuna ukubonisa ukuba amaXhosa anemithetho yawo. Le mithetho yayimele ubomi bamaXhosa nasendleleni ababephila ngayo.
Ityala lamawele yayiyincwadi yokuqala eyaxoxa ngemithetho yamaXhosa ngendlela ecacileyo. Abafundi bayithanda ngoba yayibaxhomekele kwindlela yabo yokulawula.
Le ncwadi yaba ngumzekelo wendlela yokulawula ethi ibe ngowabantu bonke. UDon Jadu yayiyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu.
Le ncwadi yaxoxa ngendoda ethi ibe ngumzekelo wobuqhawe bamaXhosa. UDon Jadu waba ngumzekelo wobuqhawe bamaXhosa obuncinane xa befuna ukuzikhusela.

Abantu besizwe yayiyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu. Le ncwadi yaxoxa ngemibongo yabantu abahlukileyo ababephila ngexesha likaMqhayi.

Abantu besizwe yayiyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu. UMqhayi waseNtab'ozuko yayiyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu.
Le ncwadi yaxoxa ngobomi bakaMqhayi ngendlela ecacileyo kunomdla onzulu. UMqhayi wabonakalisa ubuchule bakhe ekubhaleni ngolwimi lwesiXhosa.
Le ncwadi yaba ngumthombo wamandla kwabantu baseNtla-langa. UMqhayi waseNtab'ozuko yayiyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu.
Le ncwadi yaba ngumzekelo wendlela yokuphila ethi ibe ngowabantu bonke. UMqhayi waseNtab'ozuko yayiyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu.
UBombo Mfundisi uJohn Knox Bokwe yayiyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu. Le ncwadi yaxoxa ngobomi bukaMfundisi uJohn Knox Bokwe ngendlela ecacileyo kunomdla onzulu.
UBombo Mfundisi uJohn Knox Bokwe yayiyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa ngendlela ezintle kakhulu. Abafundi bayithanda le ncwadi ngoba yayibaxhomekele kwindlela yabo yokuphila.

Ndiyakwazi ukuphila ngaphandle kwam. Isibongo: praise poetry imbongo yobuqhawe yamadoda namakhosikazi.
Imibongo: poetry imibongo emihle ebhalwe ngabantu abanolwimi. Imihobe: songs and lullabies imihobe ethi ikhuthaze abantu ngolwimi lwabo.
Isihobe: formal poem isihobe esicacileyo esixhomekeke kwimithetho yesiXhosa. Uchongo lwamagama: wordplay ukudlala ngamagama ngendlela emnandi.
Imifanekiso ntelekelelo: metaphors ukufanisa izinto ngendlela ebonakalayo. Ithoni: tone indlela umntu athetha ngayo ebopha ubuqhawe.
Imiqondiso: symbols izinto ezimele into enye. Isingqisho: rhyme ukuphindaphinda izandi ngendlela emnandi.
Injambamenti: enjambament xa umgca uqhubeka kumgca olandelayo. Umxholo: theme nto ephambili esIxoxwa ngayo.
Ubume: structure indlela incwadi ilungiselwe ngayo. Abalinganiswa: characters abantu abaxoxwa ngabo kwindaba.
Isimo sentlalo: setting indawo nlexesha lendaba. Isityilio: style indlela umbhali abhala ngayo.
Inqwaba: grammar imithetho yesiXhosa. Isigama: noun igama elichaza into.
Isenzo: verb igama elichaza into esenzekayo. Isichazi: adjective igama elichaza into ngokunyuka.
Isixhumanisi: conjunction igama elichaza into ehlanganisayo. Izanduko: prefixes amagama acala emgqubheni wesigama.
Izigidimi: suffixes amagama acala emva kwesigama. Ukuguquguquka: conjugation ukuguquka kwesigama ngexesha.
Isichazi-senzo: adverb igama elichaza indlela isenzo esenzeka ngayo. Xa kusekusa: present tense ngoku xa sikwenzayo.
Xa kusezulwini: past tense ngaphambili xa bekusenzwa. Xa kuzakuba: future tense xa kuya kube.
Imbongi: praise singer umntu othetha izwi lezizwe. Ubuntu: humanity thina singabantu ngabantu.
Imithetho: laws imithetho yesizwe. Imasiko: customs imithetho yendlela yokuphila.
Inkosi: chief umntu olawula abantu. Umkhulu: elder umntu omkhulu ngobudala.
Abantwana: children abantwana bethu. Usapho: family abantu abaxhunywe ngobuhlobo.
Ukuthetha: naming ceremony xa umntu esaziwa ngegama. Ukutshata: wedding xa abantu beqinisekisa ubuhlobo babo.
Ukufenxa: funeral xa umntu eshiywa ngabantu bakhe. Ukusenza: initiation xa umntu ephila endleleni entsha.

Ndiyakuthanda ulwimi lwesiXhosa ngoba luhle kakhulu. Umntu ngumtu ngabantu yiqiniso elingundoqo.
AbaQhawu bamaXhosa bayibulalis' ixhego ngomkhwazo. UMqhayi lowo ngumbongi omninzi imbongi yesizwe jikelele.
Ityala lamawele liyincwadi exhambisa ubuchule bamaXhosa. Imbongi yindoda ethetha izwi lezizwe ithethe ngeentloni.
Ukuthetha iqiniso kukubulala uxhego ngaphandle kwabantu. Indoda ngumuntu onendawo yakhe emhlabeni.
Abantwana basafunda esikolweni ngolwimi lwabo. Izibongo zizivakaliso zobuqhawe bomntu.
Umthetho wamaXhosa uyohlala ume nkqo njengentaba. Ndixhomeke kwimithetho yesizwe yam.
Intetho yam ibhalwe ngeqiniso ngaphandle kobuxoki. Impumelelo yethu ihambelana nokubambisana.
Ukufunda nokubhala yizakhono ezibalulekileyo. Izwe lakithi lineentylo ezintle nabantu abalungileyo.
Siyavuya ngokuba siphila kwilizwe lamaXhosa. Inkosi yethu ithanda abantu bayo ngentliziyo yonke.
Imbongi yakwaGompo yaba ngumbongi wezizwe zonke. USamson yayiyincwadi yokuqala ebhalwe ngolwimi lwesiXhosa.
UDon Jadu Ucele abantu bakuthi bahlangane. Imihobe nemibongo yimibongo emhle kakhulu.
Inzuzo yimibongo yentembeko nokuthembeka. UBukumkani bamaXhosa buhlala buphakade.
Iinkosi zakwaXhosa zazilawula ngobulungisa. Ukuxabana akulungileyo kufuneka kugqitywe.
Umbuso wezwe kufuneka ube ngowabantu bonke. Imfundo yindodla esikhulisa ngokuyiqonda.
Isikolo sisindululo esihamba phambili. Abafundi bafunda ngolwimi lwabo ngentlonipho.
Utitshala ngumntu onceda abafundi bake. Incwadi ngumthombo wolwazi olunzulu.
Ukufunda incwadi kukuvula intsapho yezazi. Imibhalo yamashumi eminyaka idlala indima ebulungileyo.
Ababhali bethu babhala ngobuchule nokulungela. Ulwimi lwesiXhosa luneenzululwazi ezininzi.
Iintlawulo zolwimi zixhomekeke kwindlela yokuthetha. Ukuthetha kakuhle kukubonakalisa ubuchule bomntu.
Imibongo yethu igcina ubomi bethu busel' ekukhululweni. Imbongi ngumntu onomdla wokuthetha ngeentloni.
Izibongo zethu zicacisa ubomi bethu ngamazwi. Inkosi ngumntu onoxanduva lwabantu bakhe.
Abakhulu basele badlala indima ebalulekileyo. Umatshini wethu wokubhala ubhala ngolwimi lwesiXhosa.
Iinzwelwe zethu zinoxanduva lokuthetha iqiniso. Ukuthetha iqiniso kukubulala uxhego.
Indoda yethu inendawo yethu emhlabeni. Abantu bakuthi banoxanduva lokuthetha izwi.
Impumelelo yethu ihamba phambili ngokubambisana. Ukubambisana kukuthi siphile ndawonye.
Izwe lakithi liningi iintlobo zabantu. Unxweme lwethu lusel' apha kumntu ngamnye.
Imveliso yethu inoxanduva lwethu. Ulwimi lwethu lufuneka luhlala lusebenza.
Iilwimi zethu zihamba phambili ngokubambisana. Ukubhala ngolwimi lwethu kukubhala ngobomi bethu.
Indlela yokubhala ilungile xa ikhuthaza abantu. Incwadi yethu igcina ubomi bethu busel' ekuphileni.
Nangani ndingengcali kwathi ni yamthetho ndinawo noko amanakani okuthabatha umthetho wasemaXhoseni awahluke nakancinane kowezizwe ezikhanyiselweyo.
Iintlanga eziMhlophe zithe zakufika kweli lizwe zafumana ukuthi aBantu belli lizwe baphantse ukuthabiwa ziincutfhe zomthetho bonke namasiko aba asekwe phezu kwendlela yakudala.
Yindawo yomlisela nomthinjana wasemaXhoseni ukuze ukhangele ngokucokisekileyo ukuthi iya kuthi yakuthonela iphele le ntetho nale mikhwa inesidima yakowawo kutjho nele nto ni na emveni koko.
La ke ngoko ngamazwembe-zwembe okuzama ukuxha thalaza kuloo msinga uza kuthayela isizwe siphela.
Zama ni ukuwaxhasa nani ngelenu icala ndingowenu emigudwini yesizwe S. E. Kfune Mqhayi.
UMBALI KA SAMSON.
Le ncwadi yaxoxa ngendoda ebandlululweyo ekufuneka iphinde ifumane amandla ayo ngokubuya emzimbeni nasentlizyweni.

UWele nomninawe bakhe bathetha ityala elalixoxwa ngabantu abaninzi emntwini wonke.

Le ncwadi yaba ngumzekelo wendlela yokulawula ethi ibe ngowabantu bonke.
Le ncwadi yaxoxa ngendoda ethi ibe ngumzekelo wobuqhawe bamaXhosa.

Imibongo: poetry - imibongo emihle ebhalwe ngabantu abanolwimi.
Imihobe: songs and lullabies - imihobe ethi ikhuthaze abantu ngolwimi lwabo.
Uchongo lwamagama: wordplay - ukudlala ngamagama ngendlela emnandi.

Ithoni: tone - indlela umntu athetha ngayo ebopha ubuQU.

Umxholo: theme - nto ephambili esIxoxwa ngayo.
Ubume: structure - indlela incwadi ilungiselwe ngayo.
Abalinganiswa: characters - abantu abaxoxwa ngabo kwindaba.
Isimo sentlalo: setting - indawo nlexesha lendaba.
Isityilio: style - indlela umbhali abhala ngayo.
Inqwa ba: grammar - imithetho yesiXhosa.

Isenzo: verb - igama elichaza into esenzekayo.
Isichazi: adjective - igama elichaza into ngokunyuka.
Isixhumanisi: conjunction - igama elichaza into ehlanganisayo.
Izanduko: prefixes - amagama acala emgqubheni wesigama.
Izigidimi: suffixes - amagama acala emva kwesigama.
Ukuguquguquka: conjugation - ukuguquka kwesigama ngexesha.
Isichazi-senzo: adverb - igama elichaza indlela isenzo esenzeka ngayo.
Xa kusekusa: present tense - ngoku xa sikwenzayo.
Xa kusezulwini: past tense - ngaphambili xa bekusenzwa.
Xa kuzakuba: future tense - xa kuya kube.
